### [1] [$L^2$-regularity of minimizers of anisotropic interaction functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19181)
*Tristan Bullion-Gauthier,Kai Xiao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究各向异性核 W 与一般约束势 V 构成的交互泛函极小元，证明其具有 L² 密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互泛函的极小元正则性（如密度是否属于 L²）是非局部变分与聚集模型中的核心问题，尤其在核各向异性情形下缺乏系统结论。

Method: 在 W 及其 Fourier 变换的标准假设下，对非局部变分不等式进行细致分析。

Result: 证明了所考虑泛函 E 的极小元具有 L² 密度。

Conclusion: 极小元的 L² 正则性成立，且所用非局部变分不等式分析具有独立价值。

Abstract: We consider interaction functionals of the form \begin{equation*} ν\mapsto {\mathscr E}(ν)=\int\_{{\mathbb R}^N}\int\_{{\mathbb R}^N} W(x-y)\intdν(y)\intdν(x)+\int\_{{\mathbb R}^N} V(x)\intdν(x)\text{,} \end{equation*} involving an anisotropic kernel $W$ and a general confinement potential $V$. Under standard assumptions on $W$ and its Fourier transform, we show that the minimizer of ${\mathscr E}$ has $L^2$ density. Our argument relies on a careful analysis of nonlocal variational inequalities, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [2] [The unique solvability of strong solution to the multi-dimensional nonhomogeneous incompressible two-phase magnetohydrodynamic model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19205)
*Lingxin Jiang,Fuyi Xu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究有界光滑区域上带Landau势的非齐次不可压缩两相磁流体模型的初边值问题，构造了三维局部强解与二维大初值全局强解，并证明了唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非齐次不可压缩两相磁流体模型耦合了流体、磁场与相场演化，具有强非线性与奇异性，在带Landau势情形下强解的存在唯一性尚待建立。

Method: 在$\mathbb{R}^d(d=2,3)$有界光滑区域上研究初边值问题，允许密度有界且初值任意大；借助加权能量估计、可积性迁移方法与Lagrangian方法处理耦合非线性并证明唯一性。

Result: 三维情形下得到局部时间强解的存在性；二维情形下对任意大初值与有界密度得到全局强解的存在性；并进一步证明了该强解的唯一性。

Conclusion: 该工作为带Landau势的非齐次不可压缩两相磁流体模型建立了强解的存在性与唯一性理论，其中二维大初值全局适定性与唯一性刻画是本结果的核心贡献。

Abstract: The present paper studies the initial-boundary value problem of the nonhomogeneous incompressible two-phase magnetohydrodynamic model with Landau potential in a bounded smooth domain in $\mathbb{R}^d$($d = 2, 3$). More precisely, we construct the existence of local in time in three dimension and the global existence of strong solution in two dimension with arbitrary large data and bounded density. In addition, the uniqueness of the solution is proved through the weighted energy estimates, the shift of integrability method and Lagrangian approach.

</details>


### [3] [The Cauchy problem for logarithmic Schr{ö}dinger equation with a perturbation of power law nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19217)
*Mohamed Bensaid*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究含对数项与局部多项式非线性项耦合的非线性Schrödinger方程的Cauchy问题，在亚临界情形下于能量空间W_1(R^d)中构造解，讨论L^2临界情形下全局存在的充分条件，并在空间Σ_α中建立更一般的Cauchy理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性Schrödinger方程中对数非线性与局部多项式非线性耦合的情形尚缺乏系统的Cauchy理论研究，尤其是能量空间W_1(R^d)与空间Σ_α中的适定性问题未被充分探讨。

Method: 针对含对数项与多项式增长局部非线性项耦合的NLS方程Cauchy问题，采用能量空间W_1(R^d)框架，在亚临界情形下构造解；并引入空间Σ_α建立更一般的Cauchy理论。技术手段未在摘要中具体说明。

Result: 在亚临界情形下，于能量空间W_1(R^d)中成功构造了方程的解；给出了L^2临界情形下各空间中全局存在的一个充分条件；并在空间Σ_α中建立了更一般的Cauchy理论。

Conclusion: 该工作将对数非线性与局部多项式非线性耦合的NLS方程Cauchy问题拓展到能量空间W_1(R^d)与空间Σ_α，为后续适定性与全局存在性研究提供了框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the Cauchy problem for the nonlinear Schr{ö}dinger equation with a nonlinearity combining a logarithmic term and a local nonlinearity which has polynomial growth. Our main result is the construction of solutions in the energy space $W_1(R^d)$ in the subcritical case. We also discuss a sufficient condition for global existence in the $L^2$-critical case in each of these spaces. Moreover, we establish a more general Cauchy theory in the space $Σ_α$, as introduced without details in [6].

</details>


### [4] [Long-time dynamics for the magnetic Schr{ö}dinger equation on the torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19224)
*Julien Lechaux*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究环面上磁Schrödinger流的长时间半经典测度，证明在有效磁力的几何非零假设下存在两个时间阈值，并得到量子唯一遍历性的动力学形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨磁Schrödinger流在长时间尺度下半经典测度的行为，尤其是量子唯一遍历性（QUE）是否以动力学形式成立。

Method: 在平坦二维环面 $\mathbb T^2$ 上考察时间依赖的半经典测度，对有效磁力施加几何非零假设，并分析不同时间尺度 $\tau_h$ 下的极限测度。

Result: 识别出两个时间阈值：若 $\tau_h \gg h^{-1/2}$，则位置边缘测度为 $\mathbb T^2$ 上的归一化Lebesgue测度；若 $\tau_h \gg h^{-1}$，则动量边缘测度在每个正则能级集上为该曲线上的归一化不变测度。

Conclusion: 在几何非零假设下，磁Schrödinger流表现出动力学形式的量子唯一遍历性，且位置与动量边缘测度分别在不同时间阈值后趋于相应的自然极限测度。

Abstract: We investigate time-dependent semiclassical measures associated with the magnetic Schrödinger flow on the flat two-dimensional torus in long-time regimes. Under a geometric nonvanishing assumption on an effective magnetic force, we identify two thresholds and prove a dynamical form of quantum unique ergodicity for such systems. If the time scale $τ_h \gg h^{-1/2}$, then the configuration marginal is the normalized Lebesgue measure on $\mathbb T^2$. In addition, if $τ_h \gg h^{-1}$, then the momentum marginal is, conditionally on each regular level set, the normalized invariant measure on that curve.

</details>


### [5] [A constructive approach to range characterization of spherical mean transform in odd dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19481)
*Pradipta Chatterjee,Venkateswaran P. Krishnan,Abhilash Tushir*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对奇数维Euclidean空间中球面平均变换（SMT）的值域刻画问题，给出了此前所提出对称性条件的直接构造性充分性证明，无需依赖已有的值域刻画结果，并可显式重构原函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作给出了奇数维SMT值域的一个更简洁描述，即对值域中函数球谐展开系数施加若干线性常微分算子相关的对称关系。然而其充分性证明依赖于Agranovsky-Kuchment-Quinto已有的值域刻画结果。本文旨在摆脱这一依赖，提供独立且构造性的证明。

Method: 采用直接且构造性的方法证明上述对称条件的充分性：不引用任何已有值域刻画定理，直接处理球谐展开系数上的线性常微分算子对称关系。

Result: 成功给出对称性条件的直接构造性充分性证明，证明过程独立于现有值域刻画结果；并且对于满足值域条件的函数g，可显式构造出函数f，使其球面平均变换恰为g。

Conclusion: 本文为奇数维SMT值域的对称性刻画提供了自洽的构造性证明，并附带给出显式反演/重构方法，增强了该刻画的理论独立性与实用性。

Abstract: This work focuses on range characterization of spherical mean transform (SMT) in odd-dimension Euclidean space. In a recent work, a simpler description characterizing the range of SMT in odd dimensions was derived. This involved symmetry relations involving certain linear ordinary differential operators acting on the coefficients of the spherical harmonics expansion of the function in the range. The sufficiency part of this characterization was shown using an existing range characterization result due to Agranovsky-Kuchment-Quinto. In the current work, we provide a direct and constructive proof showing the sufficiency of the aforementioned symmetry conditions and we obtain this without invoking any of the existing range characterization results. As an added advantage, given a function $g$ satisfying the range conditions, our approach provides an explicit construction of a function $f$ such that the SMT of $f$ is $g$.

</details>


### [6] [New link between the fractional p-Laplacian operators and a class of McKean-Vlasov flight type processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19492)
*Houssine El Jeddaoui,Dany Nabab*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类由混合 $p$-拉普拉斯与分数阶 $p$-拉普拉斯算子驱动的非线性抛物型方程所关联的带跳 McKean–Vlasov 随机过程的存在性。作者先证明 PDE 解的存在性，再将其改写为非线性 Fokker–Planck–Kolmogorov 方程（在 $p\ge4$ 时保证解测度存在），随后借助一种新的非线性叠加原理求解相应的鞅问题，最终得到带跳 McKean–Vlasov 型 SDE 的弱解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标是建立非线性非局部算子（$p$-拉普拉斯与分数阶 $p$-拉普拉斯之混合）与带跳平均场随机过程之间的对应关系，即证明该 PDE 可由 McKean–Vlasov 型随机微分方程的概率解来表示，从而将确定性非线性演化方程与随机粒子系统的极限行为联系起来。

Method: 技术路线分三步：(1) 证明 $\partial_t u = \Delta_p u + \Delta_p^s u$ 在 $\mathbb{R}^N\times(0,\infty)$ 上解的存在性；(2) 将其改写为非线性 Fokker–Planck–Kolmogorov 方程，并在 $p\ge4$ 条件下保证其解测度存在；(3) 利用一个新的非线性叠加原理（nonlinear superposition principle）求解对应的鞅问题；(4) 由所得鞅解导出无穷小生成元为 $\Delta_p+\Delta_p^s$ "混合版本" 的 McKean–Vlasov 型带跳 SDE 的弱解存在性。

Result: 在 $p\ge4$ 的条件下，成功构造出与混合算子 $\Delta_p+\Delta_p^s$ 相关的带跳 McKean–Vlasov 随机过程；证明过程依次给出了 PDE 解、FPKE 的解测度、相应鞅问题的解，以及带跳 McKean–Vlasov SDE 的弱解的存在性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，混合 $p$-拉普拉斯型非线性抛物方程可以通过非线性 FPKE 与鞅问题的中介，对应于一个以 $\Delta_p+\Delta_p^s$ 的"混合版本"为生成元的带跳 McKean–Vlasov 型随机微分方程，从而建立了此类非局部非线性 PDE 的概率表示。非线性叠加原理是连接确定性方程与随机过程的核心工具。

Abstract: We prove the existence of a McKean--Vlasov stochastic process with jumps associated to the nonlinear parabolic equation $\partial_t u = Δ_p u + Δ_p^s u$ in $\R^N\times(0,\infty)$, where $Δ_p$ is the $p$-Laplacian and $Δ_p^s$ is the fractional $p$-Laplacian. The algorithm used is the following : first, after proving the existence of a solution for the PDE presented earlier, we rewrite it as a nonlinear Fokker-Planck-Kolmogorov equation whose solution-measure is guaranted when $p\ge4$. Then we solve the martingale problem associated to our FPKE via a new nonlinear supersition principle. Finally, thanks to the martingale solution obtained, we derive the existence of a weak solution for the McKean-Vlasov's type SDE with jumps whose infinitesimal generator is a << hybrid version >> of the operator $Δ_p+Δ_p^s$.

</details>


### [7] [Onsager's Conjecture for Ideal Magnetohydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19506)
*Matteo Giardi,László Székelyhidi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 构造了理想MHD方程的弱解，其在任意0≤γ<1/3下v,B∈C^γ，且不守恒总能量与交叉螺旋度、磁螺旋度非平凡，并给出各向异性Hölder界及沿磁力线更强正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将凸积分方法从理想流体推广到理想MHD，连接Goldreich–Sridhar与Iroshnikov–Kraichnan两种湍流标度图像，并扩展Enciso–Peñafiel-Tomás–Peralta-Salas的结果。

Method: 在Arnold意义下将解视为保体积微分同胚路径，结合经典凸积分技术与该Lie群Lie代数层面的几何构造。

Result: 对任意0≤γ<1/3得到v,B∈C^γ的弱解，不守恒总能量与交叉螺旋度，磁螺旋度非平凡；建立速度与磁场指数不同的各向异性Hölder界及沿每条磁力线的更强正则性；可接受指数将1/3、1/2与1/4标度联系起来。

Conclusion: 显著扩展了Enciso–Peñafiel-Tomás–Peralta-Salas的近期结果，并可无需Newton迭代重新证明Giri–Radu的结果。

Abstract: For any $0\leγ<1/3$ we construct weak solutions $(v,B,p)$ of the ideal MHD equations with $v,B\in C^γ(\mathbb T^3\times\mathbb R)$, which conserve neither the total energy nor the cross-helicity and have nontrivial magnetic helicity. We also establish anisotropic Hölder bounds with distinct velocity and magnetic exponents and stronger regularity along every magnetic field line. The admissible exponents connect the Goldreich--Sridhar \cite{GoldreichSridhar1995} spatial and parallel pair $1/3, 1/2$ with the $1/4$ spatial scaling of the Iroshnikov--Kraichnan weak-turbulence picture \cite{Iroshnikov1963,Kraichnan1965}. In the spirit of Arnold's formulation of ideal hydrodynamics, a solution is regarded as a path of volume-preserving diffeomorphisms; the proof is then based on the interplay between classical convex integration techniques and geometric constructions at the level of the Lie algebra of this Lie group. Our work substantially extends the recent result of Enciso, Peñafiel-Tomás and Peralta-Salas \cite{EnPePe} and can be used to reprove \cite{GiRa} of Giri and Radu without a Newton iteration.

</details>


### [8] [Global Minimality of Matrix-Valued Heteroclinic Connections between Vacuum Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19590)
*Sisi Guan,Yu Cheng*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究具有连续共轭轨道真空集的SU(5)×Z₂矩阵场模型的全局能量最小化,给出轨道间异宿连接的锐下界并构造达界的最小化子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典异宿连接研究多假设真空为孤立点;当真空集为连续共轭轨道时,最小化问题涉及轨道间距离与矩阵非交换结构,缺乏统一处理机制。

Method: 识别四次势零集为两紧致齐性真空轨道的并,构造以Frobenius距离为控制的辅助势;经逐点比较、校准不等式与一维余面积公式证明能量下界;将模型无关的距离-余面积判据与模型特定的轨道几何、多项式正性估计分离。

Result: 两参数情形下锐下界分别为4√2 μ³/(3λ)与9√2 μ³/(10h),由两个显式非阿贝尔kink达到,从而为全局最小化子;任意固定端点扇区中下确界相同,当且仅当端点为两轨道最近对时可达,非最近对通过沿真空流形的切向运动趋于不达。

Conclusion: 轨道到轨道的异宿类实现全局能量最小化,其能量等于真空流形间的退化加权距离;所提出的距离-余面积判据可推广至具对称生成真空的矩阵多势阱问题。

Abstract: We study global energy minimization for matrix-valued heteroclinic connections whose vacuum sets are continuous conjugacy orbits rather than isolated points. For two distinguished parameter regimes of a one-dimensional $\mathrm{SU}(5)\times\mathbb Z_2$ matrix field model, we first identify the full zero set of the quartic potential as the disjoint union of two compact homogeneous vacuum orbits. We then construct auxiliary potentials controlled by the Frobenius distance to these orbits. Pointwise comparison with the original potential, combined with a calibration inequality and the one-dimensional coarea formula, gives a sharp lower bound for every finite-energy connection joining the two vacuum components; no diagonality, commutativity, or symmetry-reduced ansatz is imposed on the competitors. Two explicit non-Abelian kinks attain the bound and are therefore global minimizers in the full orbit-to-orbit heteroclinic classes. Their exact wall tensions are $4\sqrt2\,μ^3/(3λ)$ and $9\sqrt2\,μ^3/(10h)$, respectively. Equivalently, the corresponding line-segment paths realize the degenerate weighted distance between the vacuum manifolds. We also resolve every fixed-endpoint sector: for arbitrary prescribed representatives on the two vacuum orbits, the energy infimum is the same wall tension, and it is attained if and only if the prescribed representatives form a closest pair of the two orbits. Nonclosest endpoint sectors therefore exhibit non-attainment through increasingly slow tangential motion along the vacuum manifolds. The proof separates a model-independent distance--coarea criterion from the model-specific orbit geometry and polynomial positivity estimates, providing a reusable mechanism for matrix-valued multiwell potentials with symmetry-generated vacua.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性波方程的非线性系数（弹性参数）能否由边界测量唯一反演，是地球物理与无损检测中的核心反问题。已有工作多集中于线性情形，对含二次非线性项的三维各向同性弹性波方程的参数唯一可辨识性尚不明确，因此本文研究该反问题。

Method: 考虑三维各向同性介质中带至多二次非线性项的弹性波方程；在波速非陷获（non-trapping）假设下，利用边界测量构造并分析相关反问题，讨论六个弹性参数的唯一可辨识性，并对最后一个参数在一般（generic）设定下进行论证。

Result: 六个弹性参数中有五个可由边界测量唯一确定；剩余的第六个参数在一般设定下也能被确定。

Conclusion: 在非陷获波速条件下，三维各向同性非线性弹性波方程的弹性参数基本可由边界测量唯一反演，从而为非线性弹性成像的可辨识性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We consider the elastic wave equation in three-dimensional isotropic medium with up to quadratic nonlinear terms. We show that five out of the six elastic parameters can be uniquely determined by boundary measurements under the assumption that the wavespeeds are non-trapping, while the last one can also be determined in a generic setting.

</details>


### [10] [Determination of the potential by a fixed angle scattering data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19698)
*Suliang Si*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明紧支势可由固定角度远场散射信息唯一确定，解决了Rakesh与Salo提出的公开问题，核心工具为新型Carleman估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定角度散射反问题中，能否由单一固定角度下的远场数据唯一重构紧支势，此前是Rakesh与Salo提出的公开难题，尚无定论。

Method: 建立了一个新颖的Carleman估计，并沿用Bukhgeim与Klibanov提出的将Carleman估计用于反问题求解的思路进行论证。

Result: 证明了紧支势可由固定角度下的远场模式被唯一确定，从而解决了该公开问题。

Conclusion: 固定角度散射问题的唯一性得以确立，表明Carleman估计方法在该类逆散射问题中具有有效性与适用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the fixed angle scattering problems raised by Rakesh and Salo. We show that a compactly supported potential is uniquely determined by the far field pattern at a fixed angle, which is an open problem. Our method is based on a novel Carleman estimate and the ideas introduced by Bukhgeim and Klibanov on the use of Carleman estimates for inverse problems.

</details>


### [11] [Quasilinear Equations with Exponential Reactions and Measure Data on Carnot Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19704)
*Shiguang Ma,Zijian Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在Carnot群上研究非负解的存在性与Wolff位势逐点界，针对含指数型非线性项 $H_l(\alpha u^\beta)$ 与Radon测度 $\mu$ 的方程，分别处理次临界与临界情形。


<details>

Abstract: Blow-up in the self-dual Chern--Simons--Higgs equation has a rich multiscale structure, but the detailed theory has largely focused on the simple regime, where one entire profile resolves each concentration point. By contrast, non-simple blow-up is analytically more delicate: several interacting regular cores must be resolved simultaneously as they collapse toward a single vortex while remaining separated on their own scales. Under a structural condition on the autonomous nonlinearity, we obtain uniqueness, up to translations, of the nonnegative autonomous profile with connected positivity set and a corresponding energy gap below the two-profile threshold. Abstract: We establish interior $C^{1,α}$ regularity estimates, for some $α> 0$, for fractional $p$-caloric functions when $p$ is in the range $p \in (2,2/(1-s))$. Abstract: In this paper, we use the Green's function method to study the stability of solutions to micropolar equations in the whole space. We propose a dominant factor extraction method to overcome this difficulty. Abstract: The large time behavior of global strong solutions is investigated for the one-dimensional planar compressible MHD system in unbounded domains. Constant positive viscosity is adopted, and heat conductivity is taken proportional to a nonnegative power of temperature. The Cauchy problem on the whole real line and two initial-boundary value problems on the half-line are considered. For these half-line problems, the boundary is thermally insulated, and either homogeneous Neumann or homogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions are imposed on the magnetic field. This estimate demonstrates that the deviation between the squared magnitude of the magnetic field and its spatial average admits a square-root-in-time bound, which in turn implies that the specific volume admits uniform upper and positive lower bounds in both space and time. Abstract: We study the simultaneous identification of the Lamé parameters $λ$ and $μ$ in three-dimensional static isotropic elasticity. Abstract: Viscosity solutions of eikonal equations need not enjoy regularity beyond Lipschitz continuity. In contrast, differentiable solutions exhibit stronger structural properties. In the Euclidean setting, building on observations of Caffarelli and Crandall (Comm. Partial Differential Equations $\textbf{35}$ (2010), 391--414), one finds that differentiable solutions of the eikonal equation $\| Df \| \equiv 1 \,\, \text{in } \mathcal{U} \subset \mathbb{R}^d$ are of class $C^{1, 1}_{\mathrm{loc}}(\mathcal U)$. Moreover, if $\mathcal{U} = \mathbb{R}^d$, then $f$ must be affine. This manuscript revisits these phenomena in the setting of (finite-- and infinite--dimensional) Banach spaces. Our results show how the geometry of the underlying Banach space determines the regularity and affineness of Gateaux--differentiable solutions of eikonal equations. As a geometric application in finite dimensions, we characterize differentiable functions whose gradient ranges are constrained to boundaries of convex bodies.

Abstract: We study a variable-exponent double-phase Neumann problem on a domain in a metric measure space, where the boundary measure satisfies an upper codimension-\(θ\) bound for \(0\leθ<1\). Abstract: At the critical Hardy Gaussian weight for the Schrödinger equation in one space dimension on $[0,1]$, the known nonzero scalar example in the weighted $L^2$ class carries a complex-valued potential. Abstract: We prove that the full spectrum of the graph $p$-Laplacian is finite for every finite graph and every real $p>1$. This resolves the problem of finiteness of the full graph spectrum posed by Amghibech (2003). More generally, for signed weighted graph $p$-Schrödinger operators with arbitrary real potentials, we obtain bounds in terms of the numbers of vertices and positive-weight edges, uniform in all coefficients and in $p$. On $n$ vertices with $m$ positive-weight edges, the logarithms of the spectral cardinality and of the total number of connected components of the normalised eigenvector sets are $O((n+m)^2)$; the sum over the full spectrum of their rational Betti numbers is at most $\exp(C(n+m)^2)$ for an absolute constant $C$. These topological bounds extend to homogeneous eigenproblems built from arbitrary finite families of linear forms, including generalised $p$-eigenvalue problems for matrix pairs. For the uniformly weighted $K_n$ and $p\ne2$, we identify the nonconstant eigenlines with the barycentres of the coordinate-hyperplane cell decomposition of $\mathbb RP^{n-2}$ and determine their local Morse data on either side of $p=2$. A renormalised logarithmic limit describes the transition at $p=2$. Positive integer weights then separate these critical values, giving at least $(3^n-2^{n+1}+3)/2$ distinct spectral values for every fixed $p\ne2$ and every $n\geq2$. By contrast, the maximal spectral cardinality at $p=2$ is $n$; at $p=4$, the maximal spectral cardinalities in the unsigned and general classes both have exponential growth rate exactly $3$. For $n\geq3$ the same examples resolve Amghibech's extremal question. The proof combines o-minimal and Pfaffian geometry with projective $L^p$-duality, Morse theory, critical groups, and tensor eigenvalue bounds.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\operatorname{curl}\operatorname{curl}$在无界区域上的谱结构受区域几何强烈影响，已有结果多针对特定区域。本文希望系统刻画在不同几何类（准锥、准柱、角状域、带孔角状域）上$\mathcal{M}$的本性谱，并理解体积、边界平均凸性以及孔的收缩速度对谱的定性影响。

Result: 在准锥形区域中$\sigma_e(\mathcal{M})=[0,+\infty)$，而在准柱形区域中$\sigma_e(\mathcal{M})\neq\emptyset$。对具有圆形截面且边界最终为平均凸的角状域，无论其体积如何，都有$\sigma_e(\mathcal{M})=\emptyset$。对环形截面角状域，有$\sigma_e(\mathcal{M})\supseteq\sigma_e(H_V)$。对一类带双指数孔的具体指数角状域族，随孔在无穷远收缩率不同，分别出现$\sigma_e(\mathcal{M})=\emptyset$、$\sigma_e(\mathcal{M})=[\gamma^2,+\infty)$或$\sigma_e(\mathcal{M})=[0,+\infty)$三种情形。

Conclusion: $\operatorname{curl}\operatorname{curl}$算子的本性谱在无界区域上高度依赖于区域几何与拓扑：圆截面平均凸角状域的谱为空，环形截面的横向调和场支配一维有效谱，而带孔角状域中孔收缩率可决定谱的存在性与下界，从而给出谱类型的三分现象。

Abstract: We analyse the essential spectrum of ${\mathcal M} = {\operatorname{curl}} {\operatorname{curl}}$ acting on divergence-free vector fields in unbounded domains of $\mathbb{R}^3$. We show that $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = [0,+\infty)$ in quasi-conical domains and $σ_e({\mathcal M}) \neq \emptyset$ in quasi-cylindrical domains. For horn-shaped domains with circular cross-section and eventually mean-convex boundary, we establish that $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = \emptyset$, independently of their volume. For horns with annular cross-section, the transverse normal harmonic field determines an effective one-dimensional Schrödinger operator $H_V$ with $σ_e({\mathcal M}) \supseteq σ_e(H_V)$. Finally, for a concrete family of perforated exponential horns with a double-exponential hole, we show that depending on the rate of shrinking of the hole at infinity, either $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = \emptyset$, or $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = [γ^2, +\infty)$, or $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = [0,+\infty)$.

</details>

Abstract: Let $p_n$ be the $n$th prime, and let $r,s\ge2$ be fixed multiplicatively independent integers. We count the integers up to $x$ whose prime factors all have the form $p_{r^a s^b}$ with integers $a,b\ge0$. Our asymptotic formula for this count has relative error $o(1)$ and is explicit down to the multiplicative constant. For $(r,s)=(2,3)$ these integers are the prime codes of the ordinals below $ω^{ω^2}$, so the formula settles that case of the counting problem of Vernaeve, Vindas and Weiermann.

Abstract: In recent work, Hirschhorn and the second author defined $a_k(n)$ to be the number of partitions of $n$ wherein the even parts come in only one color, while the odd parts may be ``colored'' with one of $k$ colors for fixed $k\geq 1$. This function generalizes the classical partition function and has been of significant interest because it satisfies a number of congruences. For $q>2$ and odd $n\ge5$, we also prove that the Beelen--Montanucci curves are not covered by the Hermitian curve, extending the known result for Galois coverings. For every prime power $q$ and $n\ge4$, we also give an explicit automorphism subgroup of order $(q^n+1)q(q^2-1)$.

Abstract: Let $D$ be an odd fundamental discriminant, with $D=1$ permitted, and let $r\geq 1$ be fixed. We prove that, for every sufficiently large integer $\ell$ satisfying $(-1)^\ell D>0$, the first $r$ traced diagonal Rankin--Cohen brackets \[
  \mathrm{Tr}_1^{|D|}[G_{\ell-2e,D},G_{\ell-2e,D}]_{2e},
  \qquad 1\leq e\leq r, \] are linearly independent in $S_{2\ell}(SL_2(\mathbb Z))$. Here $G_{k,D}$ is the Eisenstein series of weight $k$, level $|D|$, and nebentypus $χ_D$. The Petersson formula of Kayath, Lane, Neifeld, Ni, and Xue then implies that at least $r$ normalized Hecke eigenforms $f\in S_{2\ell}(SL_2(\mathbb Z))$ satisfy $L(f\otimesχ_D,\ell)\neq 0$. Abstract: In the present paper, we study finite subgroups of the outer automorphism groups of the absolute Galois groups of mixed-characteristic local fields from the point of view of mono-anabelian geometry. We shall refer to a finite subgroup of the outer automorphism group of the absolute Galois group of a mixed-characteristic local field as quasi-geometric if its inverse image in the automorphism group of the corresponding absolute Galois group is isomorphic to the absolute Galois group of some mixed-characteristic local field. It is well-known that the image of the natural injective homomorphism from the automorphism group of a mixed-characteristic local field to the outer automorphism group of the associated absolute Galois group yields a typical example of a quasi-geometric subgroup. The main result of the present paper asserts the existence of non-quasi-geometric finite subgroups of the outer automorphism groups of the absolute Galois groups of mixed-characteristic local fields. This result shows that the set of quasi-geometric subgroups of the outer automorphism group of the absolute Galois group of a mixed-characteristic local field cannot be characterized, in general, as the set of all finite subgroups of the outer automorphism group.

Abstract: We study logarithmic Mellin integrals attached to a reciprocal quadratic rational function. For the principal half-weight example we determine the minimal quartic, complete finite branch locus, minimal differential equation, polynomial recurrence, and coefficient asymptotics. Throughout the saddle region $c>1$, $-2\sqrt c<b<0$, positive hyperbolic formulas for real $0<a<1$ give determinate Hausdorff moment sequences, strict total positivity, and monotone quotient limits. For real $\vert{}a\vert{}<1$, a general coefficient asymptotic in the same region shows that one maximum controls both the sampling radius and the high logarithmic moments. On the fixed-critical locus, two-term diagonal asymptotics give a normalized product tending to $π$; division by the displayed correction $1+γ/M$ gives an $O(M^{-2})$ approximation.

We show that $\{1^{σ(1)},2^{σ(2)},\ldots,n^{σ(n)}\}$ is a complete residue system modulo $n$ for some permutation $σ$ of $\{1,2,\ldots,n\}$ if and only if $p:=n/2$ is prime and $p-1$ is squarefree. Gate III uses compression, closure, and the restricted odd Weil criterion. The simultaneous common-cut theorem keeps the parent-ground and transverse budgets separate before the common infimum, while the fold identity leaves a non-negative remainder. Starting from the rigorously certified endpoint $Y=7$, Schur induction yields positivity at the integer endpoints, zero-extension gives non-negativity at every finite support radius, and closure together with the restricted odd Weil criterion yields the Riemann hypothesis. Abstract: We prove the conjecture of Huang, Jiang, and Oblomkov (HJO) giving a geometric extension of the Rogers--Ramanujan and Andrews--Gordon identities for every torus-knot singularity $X^a=Y^b$ with coprime $1<a<b.$ For a prime power $q$, let $\mathcal{NC}_n^{a,b}(\mathbb F_q)$ denote the set of pairs of commuting nilpotent $n\times n$ matrices $(A,B)$ over $\mathbb F_q$ satisfying $A^a=B^b$. We establish the threefold equality between their normalized counts, the HJO $q$-series $Z_{a,b}$, and the explicit infinite product $P_{a,b}$: \[ \underbrace{\vphantom{\Bigg|} \prod_{m\geq1}(1-q^{-m}) \Biggl(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty} \frac{\lvert\mathcal{NC}_n^{a,b}(\mathbb F_q)\rvert} {\lvert\operatorname{GL}_n(\mathbb F_q)\rvert}\Biggr) }_{\text{point count}} = \underbrace{\vphantom{\Bigg|}Z_{a,b}(q^{-1}) }_{\text{\(q\)-series}} = \underbrace{\vphantom{\Bigg|}P_{a,b}(q^{-1}) }_{\text{infinite product}}. \] Our main result is a stronger finite identity: the rank $N$ HJO sum equals $(q;q)_N$ times the generating function for balanced cylindric partitions with entries bounded by $N$. Taking $N\to\infty$ yields the HJO conjecture. The proof combines the compositional rational shuffle theorem of Bergeron--Garsia--Leven--Xin and Mellit with a multiplicativity theorem for slope operators and a determinantal model for bounded cylindric partitions, linked by a common $q$-difference equation. The finite identity and the HJO conjecture have been formalized in Lean by AxiomProver, conditional on two stated literature inputs.

Conclusion: 本文通过遍历论途径统一并推广了关于 x+y=⌊α(n)⌋ 型加性方程的密度与划分结果，给出了多项式增长 Hardy 域函数可解性的完整刻画，并展示了该方法在更一般的双集合问题、线性组合方程以及素数子集情形中的适用性。

Abstract: Motivated by questions and results of Erdős, Sárközy and Sós (1989), and Khalfalah and Szemerédi (2006), we introduce an ergodic approach to additive problems concerning solutions of equations of the form \[ x+y=\lfloorα(n)\rfloor,\quad x,y\in A,n\in\mathbb{N} \] where $α(t)$ is a sufficiently regular function, such as a polynomial or a logarithmico-exponential function of polynomial growth (e.g. $α(t)=t^{5/2}+t^2\log t$), and $A$ is either a given set of positive upper density, or a cell of a prescribed finite partition of $\mathbb{N}$. We give a complete description of the Hardy field functions of polynomial growth for which these partition and density problems are always solvable.
  The ergodic approach also applies to the more general problem of finding $x\in A_1,y\in A_2$, where $A_1,A_2$ are two given sets of positive density. This in turn allows us to treat equations of the form $kx+ly=\lfloorα(n)\rfloor$, where $k,l\in\mathbb{Z}$.
  Some of our results also hold true when $A$ is a subset of the primes with positive relative upper density.

</details>

This estimate is uniform in the truncation parameter and extends the subcritical bound of Hoang, Moen, and Perez for $T_Ω$ to $T^*_Ω$. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结果表明谱的平面Lebesgue测度为零的次正常算子是正常的，特别地紧次正常算子是正常的。作者希望将此类结构与谱现象推广到多变量情形，即sum-hyponormal $d$-元组，以考察高维非交换算子理论中相应的正规性判据。

Method: 定义满足 $\sum_{j=1}^d [T_j^*, T_j]\geqslant 0$ 的交换 $d$-元组为sum-hyponormal元组；分析紧算子情形下的谱结构与分解；对具有交换虚部的sum-hyponormal元组建立Putnam不等式的多变量版本；利用Taylor谱和复线性多项式 $s$（系数交替为 $1$ 与 $i$）刻画谱测度条件。

Conclusion: 本文将单算子次正常理论的紧性分解、有限维正规性以及Putnam不等式推广到sum-hyponormal $d$-元组框架，并给出了以Taylor谱和多项式 $s$ 的平面测度为零为条件的正规性判据；同时指出多变量情形与单算子的本质差异，即不必为正常型。

Abstract: It is well known that every hyponormal operator on a complex Hilbert space whose spectrum has planar Lebesgue measure zero is normal. In particular, every compact hyponormal operator on a complex Hilbert space is normal. In this paper, we investigate analogous structural and spectral phenomena for so-called sum-hyponormal $d$-tuples on a complex Hilbert space $\mathcal H$, namely, commuting $d$-tuples ${\bf T}=(T_1, \ldots, T_d)$ satisfying $\sum_{j=1}^d [T^*_j, T_j] \geqslant 0$. We show that every sum-hyponormal $d$-tuple of compact operators decomposes as the direct sum of a normal $d$-tuple and a quasinilpotent sum-hyponormal $d$-tuple. As a consequence, every sum-hyponormal $d$-tuple on a finite-dimensional Hilbert space is normal. In contrast to the single-operator case, a sum-hyponormal $d$-tuple need not be normaloid. Nevertheless, we establish a multivariable analogue of Putnam's inequality for sum-hyponormal $d$-tuples with commuting imaginary parts. As a consequence, we prove that ${\bf T}$ is normal whenever the planar Lebesgue measure of $s(σ({\bf T}))$ is zero, where $σ({\bf T})$ denotes the Taylor spectrum of ${\bf T}$ and ${s}$ is the complex-linear polynomial with alternating coefficients $1$ and $i$.

</details>

Conclusion: 无迹条件不仅是矩阵可表示为交换子的充要条件，而且可实现范数可控（绝对常数界）的定量形式，为交换子表示提供了稳定且定量的刻画。

Abstract: We prove that every traceless real or complex matrix $A$ can be written as $A=BC-CB$, with factors of the same size and over the same field satisfying $\|B\|\,\|C\|\le K\|A\|$, where $K$ is an absolute constant. Abstract: Assuming Jensen's diamond principle, we construct an infinite compact zero-dimensional space $K$ such that $C_p(K)$ has no infinite-dimensional Hausdorff separable linear quotient. ### [73] [Local Matrix Muckenhoupt Weights and Quantitative Weighted Inequalities Achieving Global Best Known Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19597)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵权理论中，全局Muckenhoupt矩阵权的实变性质与算子定量有界性已有丰富结果，但局部矩阵权情形尚缺乏系统研究。局部权与算子的"局部性"使得权的尺度与算子尺度不易匹配，且局部权与全局权的关系不明确。本文旨在建立局部矩阵权的实变理论框架，澄清其与全局权的关系，并给出多类算子在其加权空间上的定量有界性，进而应用于Schrödinger算子的Riesz变换。

Method: 首先给出局部Muckenhoupt矩阵权$W$的多种实变性质；引入新延拓性质（extension property）以厘清局部权与全局权的关系；建立最优尺度提升性质（optimal scale lifting property）以平衡权与算子的局部性。在此基础上，分别处理局部（分数）极大算子、局部分数积分算子、局部Haar平方函数及指数衰减Calderón–Zygmund算子在$L^p(W)$上的定量有界性。最后将结果应用于Schrödinger算子$-\Delta+m^2I$（$m$充分大）关联的Riesz变换。

Result: 获得了局部矩阵权的一系列实变性质，建立了若干算子在局部矩阵加权Lebesgue空间$L^p(W)$上的定量有界性：局部极大算子在$p\in(1,2]$时取得尖锐定量界；局部分数积分算子取得与全局最佳已知指数相匹配的定量界（标量情形已知尖锐）。作为应用，得到Schrödinger算子$-\Delta+m^2I$（$m\in(0,\infty)$充分大）的Riesz变换在$L^p(W)$上的定量有界性，且当$p=2$时定量界恰为$[W]_{\mathscr{A}^{\operatorname{loc}}_2(r)}^{3/2}$。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了局部Muckenhoupt矩阵权的实变理论，通过新延拓性质与最优尺度提升性质，成功刻画了局部权与全局权的关系并协调了权与算子的局部性，从而获得多类算子的定量有界性。这些结果为局部矩阵权分析提供了统一框架，并将结论推广至Schrödinger算子Riesz变换，其中$p=2$时的尖锐量化界$[W]_{\mathscr{A}^{\operatorname{loc}}_2(r)}^{3/2}$尤为显著。

Abstract: In this article, we give various real-variable properties of local Muckenhoupt matrix weights $W$ and establish the quantitative boundedness of several operators on local matrix-weighted Lebesgue spaces $L^p(W)$, including local (fractional) maximal operators, local fractional integral operators, local Haar square functions, and Calderón--Zygmund operators with exponential decay. For the local maximal operators, we obtain the sharp quantitative bounds when $p\in(1,2]$, while, for local fractional integral operators, we obtain the quantitative bounds matching the global best known exponents, whose scalar case is known to be sharp. The key used strategies include giving a new extension property (which can clarify their relationships with global ones) and an optimal scale lifting property (which can balance the locality of weights and operators under consideration) of local matrix weights. As an application, we establish the quantitative boundedness on $L^p(W)$ of the Riesz transform associated with Schrödinger operators $-Δ+m^2I$ with $m\in(0,\infty)$ being large enough, whose quantitative bound when $p=2$ is precisely $[W]_{\mathscr{A}^{\operatorname{loc}}_{2}(r)}^{\frac 32}$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Molár提出关于C*-代数上Fischer–Muszély映射的刻画问题，此前结果多依赖单射性、连续性或限制在特定代数类（如含单位、非例外情形），缺乏对任意JB代数（含非单位与例外代数）的统一处理。

Abstract: We prove that every surjective Fischer--Muszély map between positive cones of arbitrary JB-algebras is additive and positively homogeneous, and extends uniquely to a bounded positive linear surjection. In particular, this gives a complete affirmative answer to Molnár's problem for arbitrary $C^*$-algebras. The theorem includes nonunital and exceptional JB-algebras and assumes neither injectivity nor continuity. The proof connects the norm-valued functional equation to positive-cone rigidity through translation invariance of an induced pseudometric. For a surjective map satisfying the Fischer--Muszély identity with uniform error $\varepsilon$, we also construct a unique bounded positive linear map $L$ at uniform distance at most $3\varepsilon$. The set $L(A_+)$ is dense in $B_+$, although $L$ need not be surjective, even when the original map is continuous and bijective. For bijective FM maps, we obtain weighted Jordan representations on the biduals. We also extend the characterization of surjective norm-sum preservers to JB-algebras and characterize norm arithmetic-midpoint and harmonic-mean identities on positive invertible cones of unital JB-algebras.

</details>

*Pengcheng Tang,Huayou Xie*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文针对广义Cesàro算子$\mathcal C_{\mu,\gamma}$在Hardy空间$H^p\to H^q$上的有界性与紧性，给出了完整且精确的刻画，覆盖$0<p,q\le\infty$全范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Cesàro算子及加权的广义Cesàro算子在函数空间上的映射性质是算子理论的核心问题，但此前结果往往对参数$\gamma$或指标范围$p,q$有限制。作者希望建立不依赖$\gamma$限制、覆盖包括端点$H^\infty$在内的完整$H^p\to H^q$有界性分类。

Method: 以$[0,1)$上的有限正Borel测度$\mu$和$\gamma>0$定义广义Cesàro算子$\mathcal C_{\mu,\gamma}$；利用测度$\mu$的Carleson型条件、函数$F_{\mu,\alpha}=\mathcal C_{\mu,\alpha}(1)$与矩序列$\mu_n$，将算子有界性/紧性转化为等价的可测度条件、$H^r$隶属条件及矩型级数条件进行证明。

Result: 当$0<p<q<\infty$、$0<p=q<1$以及$0<p\le1,q=\infty$时，$\mathcal C_{\mu,\gamma}:H^p\to H^q$有界等价于$\mu$为$(\gamma+1/p-1/q)$-Carleson测度，且对$\gamma$无额外限制。当$1\le q<p\le\infty$时，令$1/r=1/q-1/p$，有界与紧等价于$\int_0^1(\mu([t,1))/(1-t)^{\gamma-1/r})^r\,\frac{dt}{1-t}<\infty$，也等价于$F_{\mu,\gamma}\in H^r$和$\sum_{n\ge0}(n+1)^{r\gamma-2}\mu_n^r<\infty$。当$1<p<\infty$时，$H^p\to H^\infty$的有界与紧由移位条件$F_{\mu,\gamma+1}\in H^{p'}$刻画，其中$p'=p/(p-1)$。

Conclusion: 论文完整给出了广义Cesàro算子$\mathcal C_{\mu,\gamma}$在Hardy空间$H^p$与$H^q$之间（$0<p,q\le\infty$）的有界性分类，并统一了多种等价刻画。

Abstract: Let $μ$ be a finite positive Borel measure on $[0,1)$ and let $γ>0$. We establish sharp mapping criteria for the generalized Cesàro operator \begin{equation*}
  \mathcal C_{μ,γ}f(z)
  =\sum_{n=0}^\infty μ_n
  \left(\sum_{k=0}^n
  \frac{Γ(n-k+γ)}{Γ(γ)(n-k)!}a_k\right)z^n,\qquad z\in \mathbb{D},\end{equation*}
  between Hardy spaces, including the source and target $H^\infty$ endpoints. For $0<p<q<\infty$, for $0<p=q<1$, and for $0<p\le1$ with $q=\infty$, the boundedness of $ \mathcal C_{μ,γ}: H^p \to H^q$ is equivalent to $μ$ being a $(γ+1/p-1/q)$-Carleson measure, without further restrictions on $γ$. For $1\le q<p\le\infty$, set $1/r=1/q-1/p$. In this range, boundedness and compactness are equivalent to \[
  \int_0^1
  \left(\frac{μ([t,1))}{(1-t)^{γ-1/r}}\right)^r
  \frac{dt}{1-t}<\infty. \] This condition is also equivalent to $F_{μ,γ}\in H^r$ and to $\sum_{n\ge0}(n+1)^{rγ-2}μ_n^r<\infty$, where $F_{μ,α}=\mathcal C_{μ,α}(1)$ and $μ_n=\int_{[0,1)}t^n\,dμ(t)$. For $1<p<\infty$, boundedness and compactness from $H^p$ to $H^\infty$ are characterized by the shifted condition $F_{μ,γ+1}\in H^{p'}$, where $p'=p/(p-1)$. We therefore obtain a complete boundedness classification of the generalized Cesàro operators $\mathcal C_{μ,γ}$ between Hardy spaces $H^p$ and $H^q$ for the full range $0<p,q\le\infty$.

</details>

Abstract: We consider a general, nonsymmetric Ornstein--Uhlenbeck semigroup $(\mathcal H_t)_{t>0}$. We prove an $L^p$ bound for the jump quasi-seminorms for $1 < p < \infty$ and a weak type (1,1) oscillation inequality, both with respect to the invariant measure. These results are established for the order $\varrho=2$. To do so, we analyze specific components of $(\mathcal H_t)_{t>0}$, by distinguishing between small and large values of $t$, and between local and global spatial zones. This decomposition allows us to explicitly identify which parts of the semigroup remain bounded and which are responsible for the failure of boundedness, both in a weak and in a strong sense, even with respect to Lebesgue measure.

</details>

Abstract: We give a computer-assisted proof that the universal real Grothendieck constant satisfies $K_G^{\mathbb R}\le 1.7813$. The construction combines an explicit odd Hermite threshold of degree $11$ with a signed correlation polynomial of degree $51$. A sufficient inverse-majorant inequality is certified by enclosing the scalar coefficient head through degree $301$ and bounding the entire remaining tail using a weighted Gaussian trace estimate. The finite integral is bounded on a complete interval partition; its spatial exterior is controlled analytically. Exact parameters and source code that regenerate all accepted numerical inputs accompany the paper. The bound improves both the explicit bound $1.7818666069360661$ in the recent literature and the subsequently reported, system-tested value $1.7813319810625639$.

</details>

Method: 通过加强Aujla–Bourin次可加性不等式回答Audenaert–Kittaneh问题；将Audenaert–Hiai的互反Lie–Trotter定理推广到含第三矩阵的乘积形式；利用算子不等式与谱分析方法确定最佳常数；并结合对称模(|X|+|X*|)/2证明特征值不等式。

Conclusion: 本文通过强化与推广若干经典矩阵不等式，回答了多个公开问题并确定了最佳常数，推动了矩阵分析与算子不等式理论的发展。

Abstract: This paper deals with several open questions in matrix analysis. We answer a question of Audenaert and Kittaneh by strengthening the Aujla--Bourin subadditivity inequality. We also answer questions posed by Bourin and the second author: (1) we extend the reciprocal Lie--Trotter theorem of Audenaert and Hiai to a product involving a third matrix; (2) we determine the best constants in inequalities for sums of contractions and positive block matrices, including the triangle inequality $$ |A+B+C| \leq \frac{3}{4} I + |A|+|B|+|C| $$ for three contractions $A,B,C$, where $3/4$ cannot be replaced by a smaller constant; and (3) we prove an eigenvalue inequality involving the symmetric modulus $(|X|+|X^*|)/2$ studied by Bourin, Lee, and Zhang.

</details>

Result: $\ell_\infty$ 含有 $2^{\mathfrak{c}}$ 个两两不同构的非自反 Grothendieck 子空间，且该数量达到最优；这些子空间可同时包含 $c_0$ 的典范副本并具有无穷维自反商。此外，相对 $\ell_\infty$-Grothendieck 性质不是同构不变量；对任意闭 $E\subseteq\ell_\infty$，存在与 $\ell_\infty\oplus_\infty E$ 同构的 $\ell_\infty$-Grothendieck 子空间。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak{c}=2^{\aleph_0}$. We prove that $\ell_\infty$ contains $2^{\mathfrak{c}}$ pairwise non-isomorphic non-reflexive Grothendieck subspaces. They may all be chosen to contain the canonical copy of $c_0$ and to have infinite-dimensional reflexive quotient. This is optimal and answers by the authors in 2021. We also show that the relative $\ell_\infty$-Grothendieck property depends on the specified copy of $c_0$ and is not an isomorphic invariant of the subspace. More generally, for every closed $E\subseteq\ell_\infty$ there is an $\ell_\infty$-Grothendieck subspace isomorphic to $\ell_\infty\oplus_\infty E$; taking $E=c_0$ answers a problem by González et al.

</details>

Abstract: We construct an explicit symmetric Banach norm on a function space over $(0,\infty)$ which is monotone with respect to logarithmic submajorization but is not strongly symmetric, and hence is not fully symmetric. Abstract: The $S$-matrix conjecture was formulated by Sloane and Harwit in 1976, motivated by an $A$-optimal design problem arising in spectroscopy. *Santu Bera,Shanola S. Sequeira*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文给出了单位双圆盘上 Dirichlet 型空间 $\mathcal D(\rho^{(1)},\rho^{(2)})$ 的矩序列所对应的 Lévy 测度 $\nu_{(\mathscr M_z,1)}$ 的显式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双圆盘上 Dirichlet 型空间中由矩序列 $\{\|z^\alpha\|^2\}$ 诱导的完全交替多序列，旨在获得其关联 Lévy 测度的显式刻画，深化对多变量 Dirichlet 型空间几何结构的理解。

Method: 设 $\rho^{(1)},\rho^{(2)}$ 为闭单位圆盘上的有限正 Borel 测度，利用映射 $S(z)=|z|^2$ 的推前测度 $S_*\rho^{(i)}$，在 $[0,1]^2\backslash\{(1,1)\}$ 上推导 Lévy 测度 $\nu_{(\mathscr M_z,1)}$ 的积分表示。

Result: 得到显式表示：$d\nu_{(\mathscr M_z,1)}(x)=\frac{1}{1-x_1}d((S_*\rho^{(1)})\times\delta_1)(x)+\frac{1}{1-x_2}d(\delta_1\times(S_*\rho^{(2)}))(x)$，即测度支撑在两条边界线 $x_1=1$ 或 $x_2=1$ 上，权重由推前测度与因子 $1/(1-x_i)$ 给出。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了双圆盘 Dirichlet 型空间矩序列 Lévy 测度的完整显式公式，表明其结构由两个一维推前测度通过 Dirac 测度张量分解耦合而成。

Abstract: For the Dirichlet-type space $\mathcal D(ρ^{(1)},ρ^{(2)})$ on the unit bidisc $\mathbb D^2,$ where $ρ^{(1)}$ and $ρ^{(2)}$ are finite positive Borel measures on the closed unit disc $\overline{\mathbb D},$ we show that the Lévy measure $ν_{(\mathscr M_z,1)}$ associated with the completely alternating multisequence $\left\{\|z^α\|_{\mathcal D(ρ^{(1)},ρ^{(2)})}^2 \right\}_{α\in\mathbb Z_+^2}$ admits the explicit representation
  \begin{equation*}
  dν_{(\mathscr M_z,1)}(x)=\frac{1}{1-x_1}d((S_{*}ρ^{(1)})\timesδ_1)(x)+\frac{1}{1-x_2}d(δ_1\times (S_{*}ρ^{(2)}))(x)
  \end{equation*} 
  on $[0,1]^2\backslash\{(1,1)\},$ where $S_{*}ρ^{(i)},$ $i=1,2$, denotes the pushforward measure of $ρ^{(i)}$ by the map $S:\overline{\mathbb D}\rightarrow [0,1]$ defined by $S(z)=|z|^2,$ $z\in \overline{\mathbb D},$ and $δ_1$ denotes the Dirac measure at 1.

</details>

*Juan Costa Ponce,Sergio Favier,Fabián Levis*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究Musielak-Orlicz空间中的最佳模逼近问题，引入Orlicz函数在原点的几何分类以刻画加权L¹型行为，建立存在性、变分刻画与唯一性准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有逼近理论多针对经典Orlicz/Lp空间，加权L¹型退化情形下的最佳模逼近缺乏统一框架；需要刻画Orlicz函数在原点附近的几何性质以区分不同行为。

Method: 在Musielak-Orlicz空间中引入Orlicz函数原点附近的几何分类，隔离加权L¹型行为；在最小假设下证明最佳逼近元的存在性；利用单侧导数给出极小元的变分刻画。

Result: 在最小假设下获得存在性结果；通过单侧导数得到极小元的变分特征；建立唯一性判据，包括一维Haar型定理以及高维空间中0-空间与1-空间的特征刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作为Musielak-Orlicz空间中的最佳模逼近提供了包含存在性、变分刻画与唯一性的较完整理论框架，统一并推广了加权L¹型情形下的已有结果。

Abstract: We study best modular approximation in Musielak-Orlicz spaces. A geometric classification near the origin of Orlicz functions is introduced to isolate weighted $L^1$-like behavior. We establish existence results under minimal hypotheses and provide a variational characterization of minimizers via lateral derivatives. Finally, we establish uniqueness criteria, including a one-dimensional Haar-type theorem and characterizations in $0$- and $1$-spaces in higher dimensions.

</details>


### [84] [Zero-product problem for Toeplitz operators on the Fock space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20555)
*Jie Qin*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文回答了Bauer和Le关于Fock空间上Toeplitz算子零乘积的公开问题：在$\mathbb{C}^n$($n\ge2$)上构造两个有界非径向Schwartz符号，其Toeplitz算子非零但乘积为零；在$n=1$时对$c\in(1/2,1)$构造增长不超过$Ce^{c|z|^2}$的平滑非径向符号，其扩展Toeplitz算子非零且在全体全纯多项式上乘积为零，且当$c\ge3/4$时第二个符号有界。证明用到高斯核计算、矩阵恒等式、theta函数与Fourier变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于Bauer和Le在JFA 2011提出的公开问题：在Fock空间$F^2(\mathbb C^n)$上，是否存在非零Toeplitz算子其乘积为零？此前结论多依赖径向符号，非径向情形尚不清楚，尤其在有界符号约束下问题更为微妙。

Method: 核心方法是构造性的。对$n\ge2$，直接构造两个有界非径向Schwartz符号；对$n=1$，构造两个增长不超过$Ce^{c|z|^2}$的平滑非径向符号，并借助扩展Toeplitz算子处理无界情形。技术工具包括高斯核计算、矩阵恒等式、theta函数以及Fourier变换，用于验证算子非零性与乘积为零的性质。

Result: （1）对$n\ge2$，得到两个有界非径向Schwartz符号，其Toeplitz算子在$F^2(\mathbb C^n)$上非零且乘积为零，正面回答了Bauer–Le问题。（2）对$n=1$及每个$c\in(1/2,1)$，得到两个平滑非径向符号，增长至多$Ce^{c|z|^2}$，其扩展Toeplitz算子非零且在全体全纯多项式上乘积为零；当$c\ge3/4$时第二个符号有界。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了Bauer–Le关于Fock空间Toeplitz算子零乘积的问题，并将结果从多维有界情形推广到一维无界（次高斯增长）情形，给出了增长阈值$c\in(1/2,1)$与有界性阈值$c\ge3/4$的定量刻画，为Toeplitz算子代数中零乘积现象提供了新的构造范式。

Abstract: We answer Bauer and Le's question on zero products of Toeplitz operators on the Fock space $F^2(\mathbb C^n)$[JFA, 261 (2011), 9, 2617--2640]. For $n\ge2$, we construct two bounded nonradial Schwartz symbols on $\mathbb C^n$ whose Toeplitz operators are nonzero and have zero product on $F^2(\mathbb C^n)$. For $n=1$ and each $c\in(1/2,1)$, we construct two smooth nonradial symbols of growth at most $Ce^{c|z|^2}$ for some constant $C>0$. Their extended Toeplitz operators in $F^2(\mathbb C)$ are nonzero and have zero product on all holomorphic polynomials. Moreover, the second symbol is bounded when $c\geq3/4$. Our proofs use Gaussian kernel calculations, matrix identities, theta functions and Fourier transform.

</details>


### [85] [Normal-Direction Energy and Fourier Restriction for Convex Planar Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20643)
*Vicente Vergara*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究紧凸C²平面曲线的Fourier延拓问题，需要精细刻画曲率退化导致的“平点”与“非退化”行为之间的过渡，并给出与该几何结构相匹配的局部L⁴估计及加权估计；对单项式曲线还希望确定Frostman维数意义下的临界阈值与sharp增长率。

Method: 以Gauss映射组织质量 |f|²dσ，构造法向分布测度 ν_f=N_#(|f|²dσ)；用turning测度 dμ_κ=κdσ 通过 r_R(ξ)μ_κ(B_Γ(ξ,r_R(ξ)))≍R^{-1} 定义终端切向尺度并导出角分辨率 ρ_R(ξ)=R^{-1}/r_R(ξ)；在μ_κ倍加假设下证明 r_R 是曲线可线性化到精度R^{-1}的最大尺度；据此定义法向能量并建立终端分解与横向几何，用于局部L⁴估计；对 γ_k 显式求尺度并用ν_f的角关联给出多尺度能量表示。

Result: 在μ_κ倍加条件下得到局部L⁴ Fourier延拓估计及其加权变体；对 γ_k(t)=(t,t^k) 显式给出终端尺度并给出能量关于ν_f角关联的多尺度表示；在ν_f满足s维Frostman条件下得到增长率图景，临界阈值 s_c(k)=(k-2)/(3k-4)，区分平点与非退化区域；所得增长率（含临界对数修正）在能量层面是sharp的。

Conclusion: 通过法向测度与turning测度诱导的终端尺度，可以为曲率退化曲线建立统一的多尺度Fourier延拓分析框架，获得局部L⁴与加权估计；单项式曲线的临界阈值 s_c(k)=(k-2)/(3k-4) 精确刻画了平点与非退化机制的过渡，能量层面的增长率（含对数修正）达到sharp。

Abstract: We study Fourier extension from compact convex $C^2$ planar curves by organizing the mass $|f|^2\,\mathrm dσ$ through the Gauss map. The pushforward measure \[ ν_f= N_\#\bigl(|f|^2\,\mathrm dσ\bigr) \] records its distribution in normal directions. The turning measure $\mathrm dμ_κ=κ\,\mathrm dσ$ determines an intrinsic terminal tangential scale $r_R(ξ)$ through \[ r_R(ξ)\, μ_κ\bigl(B_Γ(ξ,r_R(ξ))\bigr) \asymp R^{-1}, \] and hence a position-dependent angular resolution $ρ_R(ξ)=R^{-1}/r_R(ξ)$. Under a doubling hypothesis on $μ_κ$, $r_R$ is, up to structural constants, the largest scale on which the curve can be linearized to precision $R^{-1}$. These scales define a normal-direction energy, and the associated terminal decomposition and transverse geometry yield local $L^4$ Fourier extension estimates and weighted variants.
  For the monomial curves $γ_k(t)=(t,t^k)$, $k\geq3$ real, the terminal scales are explicit and the energy admits a multiscale representation in terms of angular correlations of $ν_f$. Under an $s$-dimensional Frostman condition on $ν_f$, this yields a growth diagram with critical threshold \[ s_c(k)=\frac{k-2}{3k-4}, \] separating the flat-point and nondegenerate regimes. The resulting rates, including the critical logarithmic correction, are sharp at the energy level.

</details>


### [86] [A Framework for Supervised and Unsupervised Learning via Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20792)
*Frédéric Protin*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 提出基于嵌套RKHS的统一学习框架，用于密度估计与二分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定RKHS中能利用计算结构，但受限于该空间的逼近能力；而完整L²空间逼近能力强但缺乏计算便利。需兼顾二者。

Method: 引入嵌套RKHS——并集在环境L²中稠密的递增RKHS序列。先在固定RKHS中建立经验平均的强收敛结果（空间连续嵌入L²）；再用嵌套结构结合稠密性，通过正交投影、对角选择及经验风险最小化进行构造。

Result: 密度估计：构造目标密度正交投影的显式经验估计，证明几乎必然收敛，对角选择得到依L²几乎必然收敛的估计序列。二分类：证明经验最小化子的一致性，获得到Bayes目标的L²收敛，并给出超平面与加权距离的几何解释。

Conclusion: 嵌套RKHS将有限层的计算结构与L²的逼近能力结合，为监督与无监督学习提供统一的可证明收敛框架，且总体风险有误分类概率的直接概率解释。

Abstract: We introduce a unified framework for supervised and unsupervised learning based on reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHSs). We introduce the notion of a nested RKHS, namely an increasing sequence of RKHSs whose union is dense in an ambient $L^2$-space. This construction makes it possible to exploit the computational structure of RKHS methods at each finite approximation level while retaining the approximation power of the full $L^2$-space.
  We first study statistical learning in a fixed RKHS. In both unsupervised and supervised settings, we establish strong convergence results for empirical averages of random elements taking values in an RKHS continuously embedded in $L^2$. We then use the nested RKHS structure to combine these statistical convergence results with the density of the union of the RKHSs in the ambient $L^2$-space. We apply this construction to probability density estimation and binary Bayesian classification. For density estimation, we construct explicit empirical estimators of the orthogonal projections of the target density onto the RKHSs and establish their almost sure convergence. A diagonal selection procedure then yields a sequence of estimators converging almost surely to the target density in $L^2$.
  For binary Bayesian classification, we formulate the approximation of the Bayes target as the minimization of suitable population and empirical risk functionals. We prove consistency of the empirical minimizers and use the nested structure to obtain convergence towards the Bayes target in $L^2$. We further provide a geometric interpretation in terms of hyperplanes and weighted distances in the ambient $L^2$-space. Finally, we show that the population risk admits a direct probabilistic interpretation in terms of the probability of misclassification.

</details>

*Yuuki Kasugai*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文确定了平衡7部K_4-free图的最大可能最小度函数f(n,7,4)，填补了Lo、Treglown和Zhao关于K_4-free族研究中的唯一遗留情形：除n≡4 (mod 7)且n≥11时值为⌊30n/7⌋−1外，f(n,7,4)=⌊30n/7⌋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lo、Treglown和Zhao在研究K_4-free族时，将f(n,7,4)确定为唯一尚未解决的剩余情形。确定该函数可以完善平衡r部K_4-free图最小度极值问题的完整刻画。

Method: 方法分两步：首先将对应的三色极值δ(n,7,3)归结为一个7×3整数矩阵问题并精确求解；其次通过结构论证证明任何满足δ(G)>132n/31的平衡7部K_4-free图都是三色可染的，从而将一般情形化归为三色情形。

Result: 对所有n≥1精确确定了f(n,7,4)：一般地f(n,7,4)=⌊30n/7⌋，例外是当n≡4 (mod 7)且n≥11时，其值比⌊30n/7⌋少1。

Conclusion: 该工作补全了Lo–Treglown–Zhao关于K_4-free族最小度极值的结果，解决了唯一遗留的f(n,7,4)情形，给出了完整的精确公式及例外情形。

Abstract: For integers $n,r,t$ with $2\le t\le r-1$, let $f(n,r,t+1)$ denote the largest possible minimum degree of a balanced $r$-partite graph with parts of size $n$ and containing no copy of $K_{t+1}$. Lo, Treglown and Zhao identified $f(n,7,4)$ as the only remaining case in their treatment of the $K_4$-free family. I determine this function for every $n\ge1$. First, the corresponding three-colourable extremum $δ(n,7,3)$ is reduced to a $7\times3$ integer matrix problem and determined exactly. Second, a structural argument shows that every balanced $7$-partite $K_4$-free graph $G$ with $δ(G)>\frac{132}{31}n$ is three-colourable. Consequently, $f(n,7,4)=\lfloor30n/7\rfloor$ except when $n\equiv4\pmod7$ and $n\ge11$, where it is one less.

</details>


### [88] [Lean-Certified Infinite Counterexamples to Written on the Wall II Conjecture 194](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19195)
*Cameron Beeley*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了四参数反例族，推翻 Written on the Wall II 猜想 194；其主子族满足该不等式且取等号，却无哈密顿路径，并在 Lean 4 中完成参数化机器验证，表明提高最小度无法修补该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Written on the Wall II 猜想 194 断言：n>1 的连通简单图若满足 alpha(G) <= 1 + l_avg(G)，则存在哈密顿路径。作者旨在检验该猜想是否成立，并考察固定最小度下界能否修复它。

Method: 构造一个四参数图族，并重点分析其双参数主子族：对整数 s>=1、t>=3，构造具有 (s+1)t^2 个顶点、独立数 t+1、l_avg(G)=t、最小度 s 的图。随后在 Lean 4 中对整个无限参数化子族进行形式化验证，给出一个全称量化定理，证明其阶、连通性、独立数、邻域平均独立数、最小度、猜想假设成立以及不可迹。

Result: 主子族对每个 s>=1、t>=3 都满足 alpha(G) <= 1 + l_avg(G) 且取等号（alpha(G)=t+1，l_avg(G)=t），但不存在哈密顿路径。该无限子族已由 Lean 4 机器验证为参数化证书，而非仅验证单个图。特例 (s,t)=(1,3) 对应 18 个顶点的图。

Conclusion: Written on the Wall II 猜想 194 被反例族推翻；由于反例对任意最小度 s 仍成立，任何固定最小度下界都无法修复该猜想。Lean 4 的参数化形式验证进一步保证了结论的可靠性。

Abstract: For a finite simple graph G, let alpha(G) denote its independence number and let
  l_avg(G) = (1 / |V(G)|) sum_{v in V(G)} alpha(G[N_G(v)])
  be the average independence number of its open neighbourhoods. Written on the Wall II Conjecture 194 asserts that every simple connected graph on n > 1 vertices satisfying alpha(G) <= 1 + l_avg(G) has a Hamiltonian path. We give a four-parameter family of counterexamples. Its principal two-parameter subfamily satisfies the proposed inequality with equality: for every pair of integers s >= 1 and t >= 3 it has (s + 1)t^2 vertices, independence number t + 1, l_avg(G) = t, and minimum degree s, but has no Hamiltonian path. This entire infinite subfamily is machine-checked in Lean 4: one universally quantified theorem certifies its order, connectivity, independence number, average neighbourhood independence, minimum degree, conjecture hypothesis, and failure of traceability. Thus no fixed lower bound on the minimum degree repairs the conjecture. The case (s,t) = (1,3) has 18 vertices, but the formal certificate is parametric rather than a verification of that one graph alone.

</details>


### [89] [A Strongly Aperiodic Monotile in Three Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19214)
*Ioannis Tsiokos*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 构造出一个有理多面体3-球 R44（Chair44），并证明用全等副本（允许反射）可铺满 R^3，且任意此类铺砌无平移周期、对称群阶至多24、同手性并具有唯一无穷嵌套"超砖"层级。证明结合书面几何论证与穷举枚举，大部分门限在 Lean 4 中经核验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Socolar 与 Taylor 提出：是否存在单个、单连通的3维原砖，仅凭形状即强制非周期性且不允许弱非周期铺砌。此前 Schmitt-Conway-Danzer 双棱柱及3维 Socolar-Taylor 砖分别存在螺旋运动或一个周期堆叠方向，未完全满足要求，故需要新的构造。

Method: 设计一个7立方体"椅子"多面体，其24个外露单位面带有微小方锥特征；基于 Six Birds emergence calculus（第3.3节）对非周期单砖现象的理解构造。关键是一个机器检验的有限测试：砖自身的接触规则在粗化下仍成立，从而使解码后的母铺砌仅服从该砖规则。证明由书面几何论证（特征迫使铺砌落在注册格点上）与穷举有限枚举组成；配套普查由两个独立实现复现，每个有限门限在 Lean 4 中核验（每个 native_decide 定理伴随一个具名编译器钩子），书面几何引理与逻辑组装亦为 Lean 定理。

Result: R44 可用全等副本（允许反射）铺满 R^3；每个此类铺砌无平移周期，对称群阶至多24；每个铺砌为同手性，并具有唯一无穷嵌套超砖层级。

Conclusion: 该工作给出一个满足 Socolar-Taylor 要求的3维单连通非周期单砖，解决了其提出的问题；证明以形式化核验（Lean 4）与机器穷举为基础，书面证明作为说明性阐述保留。

Abstract: Socolar and Taylor asked for a single, simply connected three-dimensional prototile that forces nonperiodicity by shape alone, admitting no weakly nonperiodic tiling; the Schmitt-Conway-Danzer biprism and the three-dimensional Socolar-Taylor tile admit screw motions or a periodic stacking direction. We exhibit a rational polyhedral $3$-ball $Q$, which we call Chair44 (R44): a seven-cube chair whose $24$ exposed unit panels carry tiny square-pyramid features, and prove, as a proof submission, that $Q$ admits tilings of $\mathbb{R}^3$ by congruent copies, reflections allowed, and that every such tiling has no translational period and a symmetry group of order at most $24$; every tiling is homochiral and carries a unique infinite hierarchy of nested supertiles. The solid was designed to a reading of the aperiodic-monotile phenomenon reached with the Six Birds emergence calculus (Section 3.3), and the construction turns on a single finite test, checked by machine: the tile's own contact rule survives coarsening, so that the decoded parent tiling obeys the tile's rule and no other. The proof combines a written geometric argument, that the features force every tiling onto a registered lattice, with exhaustive finite enumerations; the companion census is replayed by two independent implementations, every finite gate is kernel-checked in Lean 4 (modulo a named compiler hook per native_decide theorem), and the written geometric lemmas and the logical assembly are Lean theorems as well, so that the theorem is kernel-checked modulo the named compiler hooks; the written proofs remain as exposition.

</details>


### [90] [From one generator to loop order on the three-cube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19218)
*Jonathan Washburn,Milan Zlatanović*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究三维立方体$Q_3$上基于闭步道的幺半群$\mathcal W$，证明加法式一步读数可经转移计数分解、但交换聚合无法决定约化环路字；给出最短长度14的实例，并在有限记忆、栈记忆、势读数与四元数钟等方面建立了一系列分离结果，且结论推广到所有$n\geq3$的超立方体$Q_n$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在探究图（特别是超立方体）上闭步道的代数结构中，交换信息（如转移计数、加法式读数、占用向量）能在多大程度上恢复非交换的约化环路字，并厘清不同记忆模型（有限状态、无界栈、势函数、四元数钟）之间的表达能力边界。

Method: 以$Q_3$的1-骨架上的基于闭步道幺半群$\mathcal W$（其基本群为自由群$F_5$）为对象，分析由边权之和给出的加性一步读数，证明其经有向转移计数分解；构造具有相同转移计数但不同约化环路字的闭步道；引入约化环路字作为非交换识别商，并确定具有平凡阿贝尔化但非平凡二次交换信息的短闭步道；构造两状态读数与阶对、$k$状态读数与阶乘对的分离例子，以及无界栈恢复约化环路字的机制；研究顶点集上整数值函数对应的势读数、占用绑定的矩形性；并给出与约化字商不可比的四元数钟商。

Result: 加法式一步读数经转移计数分解；存在转移计数相同而约化环路字不同的闭步道，故交换聚合不能决定约化环路字。约化环路字给出真非交换识别商；具有平凡阿贝尔化但非平凡二次交换信息的最短闭步道在立方体边度量下长度为14。两状态读数能分离一个阶对，而对任意$k\geq1$存在显式的阶乘对不被任何$k$-状态读数分离；无界栈可在每条步道上恢复约化环路字。顶点集上整数值函数的势读数在闭步道上为零，占用绑定非矩形；一般地，占用型约束在某一历史类上矩形当且仅当占用向量在该类上为常数。声明式四分之一转四元数钟给出第二真序敏感同余，与约化字商不可比。交换恢复的障碍及最小长度14在任意$n\geq3$的超立方体$Q_n$上均成立。

Conclusion: 交换式聚合不足以刻画非交换的环路结构，而约化环路字构成有效的非交换识别商；有限状态与无界栈之间存在本质的表达能力分离；势读数与占用型约束的矩形性由占用向量的常值性精确刻画；四元数钟给出的序敏感同余与约化字商不可比。核心障碍与最小长度14具有跨维度的普适性。

Abstract: We study the monoid $\mathcal W$ of based closed walks on the $1$-skeleton of the three-dimensional cube $Q_3$. The fundamental group of this graph is the free group $F_5$. We prove that every additive one-step reading, given by a sum of edge weights in an abelian group, factors through the directed transition counts, and we exhibit two closed walks with equal transition counts and different reduced loop words. Hence commutative aggregation does not determine the reduced loop word.
  The reduced loop word gives a proper noncommutative recognition quotient. We also determine the shortest closed walk with trivial abelianization but nontrivial degree-two commutator information. Its minimum length in the cube edge metric is $14$.
  We also obtain a separation between finite and unbounded memory. A two-state reading separates an order pair, while for every $k\geq1$, there is an explicit factorial pair which no $k$-state reading separates. An unbounded stack recovers the reduced loop word on every walk.
  For integer-valued functions on the vertex set, potential readings vanish on closed walks, while occupation binding is not rectangular. More generally, every occupation-based constraint is rectangular on a class of histories if and only if the occupation vector is constant on that class.
  Finally, a declared quarter-turn quaternion clock gives a second proper order-sensitive congruence, incomparable with the reduced-word quotient. The obstruction to commutative recovery, and the minimum $14$, remain valid on every hypercube $Q_n$, $n\geq3$.

</details>


### [91] [Sumsets of Ahlfors--David regular sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19299)
*Fred Tyrrell*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了有限 (α,M)-Ahlfors–David 正则集合 A⊆[N] 的 sumset 下界 |A+A| ≥ |A|^{1+c(1-α)/log M}，并给出 [0,1] 上相应结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 Ahlfors–David 正则集合的加性结构，量化正则性对 sumset 增长的影响，并确定指数中依赖 M 的最优形式。

Method: 将多尺度熵分解与加性组合学中的逆定理相结合，证明 Ahlfors–David 正则性在每个尺度上强制产生确定的熵增益。

Result: 对任意有限 (α,M)-Ahlfors–David 正则集 A⊆[N]（0≤α<1，M≥2），存在绝对常数 c>0 使 |A+A| ≥ |A|^{1+c(1-α)/log M}；[0,1] 中情形亦有对应结论；指数中 1/log M 的依赖阶最优。

Conclusion: Ahlfors–David 正则性通过逐尺度熵增益带来可观的 sumset 增长，且所得指数依赖为最优阶。

Abstract: We prove that there is an absolute constant $c>0$ such that for every finite $(α,M)$-Ahlfors--David regular set $A\subseteq[N]$ with $0\leqα<1$ and $M\geq2$, we have the sumset estimate \[|A+A|\geq |A|^{1+c(1-α)/\log M}.\] We also prove a corresponding statement for Ahlfors--David regular sets in $[0,1]$. The proof combines a multiscale entropy decomposition with inverse results from additive combinatorics, showing that Ahlfors--David regularity forces a definite entropy gain at each scale. The dependence $1/\log M$ in the exponent is of optimal order.

</details>


### [92] [Intractable enumeration problems are like Russian nesting dolls: structural properties of monomer-dimer coverings on two-dimensional quadratic lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19231)
*Yong Kong*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究二维方格上s个二聚体覆盖的计数问题，给出了含s-1重嵌套求和的显式精确解公式，对应O(2^s)指数时间算法，其解由双指数增长的序列决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维方格上二聚体覆盖计数属于#P完全类，通常被认为难以精确求解，缺乏显式解与可控复杂度的计算方法。

Method: 揭示该计数问题精确解的内在结构，推导出包含s-1重嵌套求和的显式闭式公式。

Result: 得到精确解的显式公式，算法时间复杂度为O(2^s)；解由呈现双指数增长的序列唯一确定。

Conclusion: 尽管该问题属#P完全，通过结构分析可给出显式解，但其复杂度仍为指数级O(2^s)，解的规模随s双指数增长。

Abstract: Counting the number of coverings of $s$ dimers on two-dimensional quadratic lattices is considered as intractable and belongs to \#P-complete class. We reveal the structure of the exact solution to the problem and provide an explicit formula for it, which includes $s-1$ nesting sums. This results in an exponential time complexity of $O(2^s)$. The solution is explicitly determined by a sequence that exhibits double-exponential growth.

</details>


### [93] [Schrijver-Delsarte rigidity in association schemes and undecidability of quantum graph homomorphism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20678)
*Lorenzo Ciardo,Iris Hebbeker,Gideo Joubert,Jana Kreiß,Antoine Mottet*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了由经典度量结合方案导出的图族（如Kneser图、q-Kneser图及Johnson、Grassmann、Hamming图补图）参数化的量子同态问题是RE-完全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画量子同态问题的计算复杂性，并拓展经典度量结合方案相关图族在量子语境下的难度结果。

Method: 发展一种谱方法证明量子多态性的非语境性，结合Roberson关于射影填充数的界（基于Schrijver theta）的等式分析，以及受Erdős-Ko-Rado理论启发的结构论证。

Result: 证明了上述图族参数化的量子同态问题均是RE-完全的。

Conclusion: 通过谱方法与结构论证，确立了度量结合方案导出的图族量子同态问题的RE-完全性。

Abstract: We prove RE-completeness of the quantum homomorphism problem parameterised by families of graphs derived from the classic metric association schemes. These include Kneser graphs, $q$-Kneser graphs, and the complements of Johnson, Grassmann, and Hamming graphs. Our proof develops a spectral method for establishing non-contextuality of quantum polymorphisms. It combines an equality analysis of Roberson's bound on the projective packing number in terms of Schrijver's theta with a structural argument inspired by Erdős-Ko-Rado theory.

</details>


### [94] [A Complete Proof of the Strong Conjecture about $F$-Irregular Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19245)
*Tatiana Dovzhenok,Artem Filuta*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明：对任意阶至少为三的连通图F，存在无穷多个F-不规则图，从而证明了Strong Conjecture。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Chartrand等（1987）关于不规则图的存在性经典猜想，解决Dovzhenok、Filuta与Chuhai（2024）提出的Strong Conjecture。

Method: 以F-度（含给定顶点的、与F同构的子图数目）刻画顶点差异性，对任意阶≥3的连通图F构造无穷多个F-不规则图。

Result: 证明了对任意阶至少为三的连通图F，均存在无穷多个F-不规则图，Strong Conjecture成立。

Conclusion: F-不规则图的存在性得到基本性推广；本文为原2024年2月手稿的授权英译版本。

Abstract: A graph $G$ is called $F$-irregular if all its vertices have distinct $F$-degrees, defined as the number of subgraphs of $G$ isomorphic to a given graph $F$ and containing the respective vertex. We prove the Strong Conjecture about $F$-irregular graphs (Dovzhenok, Filuta, and Chuhai, 2024), which states that for every connected graph $F$ of order at least three, there exist infinitely many $F$-irregular graphs. Fundamentally generalizing the classical existence conjecture by Chartrand et al. (1987), this work presents an authorized English translation of our original February 2024 manuscript, which was publicly presented in full at two scientific conferences the same year.

</details>


### [95] [Posets of Hyper $(b,t)$-ary Partitions are Distributive Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19326)
*Elsa Frankel*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文将超二进制划分的分配格结果推广到超 $(b,t)$-ary 划分，证明其精化序仍构成分配格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Birkhoff 与 Ziegler 曾批评整数划分按精化序构成偏序集时结构不规整。后来 Propp、McConville 与 Sagan 证明超二进制划分偏序集是分配的，且其 join-irreducible 元素构成的偏序集与 fence posets 同构。本文希望将这一结果推广。

Method: 研究超 $(b,t)$-ary 划分，即各部分为某正整数 $b$ 的幂，且重数限制为 $t\ge b$，在精化序下考察所得偏序集，并分析其分配性与 join-irreducible 元素结构。

Result: 证明了超 $(b,t)$-ary 划分偏序集确实是分配格，给出了超二进制划分情形的推广。

Conclusion: 超 $(b,t)$-ary 划分在精化序下构成分配格，扩展了 Propp–McConville–Sagan 关于超二进制划分的结果。

Abstract: Posets of integer partitions, ordered by refinement, were criticized for unruly structural behavior by both Birkhoff and Ziegler. However, recent work of Propp, McConville, and Sagan demonstrated that such posets of hyperbinary partitions are distributive, with posets of join irreducible elements isomorphic to the well-studied class of fence posets. We provide a generalization of this result for hyper $(b,t)$-ary partitions, where all parts are powers of some positive integer $b$, and multiplicities are restricted to $t\ge b$. Then, we show that posets of hyper $(b,t)$-ary partitions are indeed also distributive lattices.

</details>


### [96] [The phylogenetic rank of a graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19372)
*Franklin Ashworth,Oliver Clarke,Jeffrey Giansiracusa,Jackson Jones,Julio Quijas-Aceves,Yue Ren*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图的Pachter-Sturmfels系统发育秩（phylogenetic rank），即等距嵌入图G所需度量树的最少数目。作者提出贪心算法与精确算法，构建了6、7个顶点所有图的系统发育秩数据库，并给出反例否定其遗传性、上界⌈n/2⌉及Pachter-Sturmfels广义四点猜想，同时证明其在1-sum和某些2-顶点-sum下的次可加性、平凡上界n-1，并对秩为1的图完成分类，构造出秩为⌈n/2⌉的无穷图族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统发育秩将系统发育树嵌入问题推广到度量树乘积，是Pachter与Sturmfels提出的核心概念。此前缺乏高效计算手段与系统的秩数据库，且若干自然猜想（遗传性、⌈n/2⌉上界、广义四点猜想）尚待检验。本文旨在开发计算算法、建立数据库并澄清这些猜想是否成立。

Method: 1) 在图G所有边长为1、度量树乘积赋以上确界范数（supremum norm）的设定下，设计贪心算法与精确算法计算系统发育秩；2) 利用算法枚举并计算6、7个顶点所有图的系统发育秩，构建完整数据库；3) 通过数据库搜索反例，并给出系统性理论证明（次可加性、上界、秩1分类与无穷图族构造）。

Result: 1) 数据库（含6、7顶点所有图）中给出反例，否定三条断言：系统发育秩具有遗传性、被⌈n/2⌉界定、以及Pachter-Sturmfels的广义四点猜想；2) 证明系统发育秩在1-sum及某些2-顶点-sum下具有次可加性；3) 证明其平凡上界为n-1（n为顶点数）；4) 完整分类了系统发育秩为1的图；5) 构造若干系统发育秩恰为⌈n/2⌉的无穷图族。

Conclusion: 系统发育秩的计算可通过贪心与精确算法有效完成，6、7顶点数据库为猜想检验提供了实证基础。遗传性、⌈n/2⌉上界与广义四点猜想均不成立；秩满足1-sum/2-顶点-sum下的次可加性与n-1平凡上界；秩1图可完全分类，并存在达到⌈n/2⌉的无穷图族。这提示系统发育秩的行为比预期更复杂，⌈n/2⌉更接近可达上界而非普遍上界。

Abstract: The Pachter-Sturmfels phylogenetic rank of a graph G is the minimal number of metric trees needed to embed G isometrically. Here, all edges of G are of length one and the product of metric trees is endowed with the supremum norm. We develop both a greedy and an exact algorithm for computing phylogenetic ranks. Using our algorithms, we construct a database of phylogenetic ranks which includes all graphs on 6 and 7 vertices. In particular, we exhibit examples disproving that the phylogenetic rank is hereditary, bounded by $\lceil \frac{n}{2} \rceil$, and a generalised 4-point conjecture by Pachter and Sturmfels. In addition, we show that the phylogenetic rank is subadditive under 1-sums and certain 2-vertex-sums, and that it is trivially upper bounded by n-1, where n denotes the number of vertices. We also provide a complete classification of graphs with phylogenetic rank 1 and construct several infinite families with phylogenetic rank $\lceil \frac{n}{2} \rceil$.

</details>


### [97] [Total-coloring of planar graphs with maximum degree 6 and without prescribed 4-cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19503)
*Enqiang Zhu,Yangyang Zhou,Jin Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了不含某些特殊4-圈的最大度为6的平面图是7-全可着色的，改进了此前"不含4-圈"的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全着色猜想（TCC）是未解决的难题，对最大度Δ(G)≤5的图已获证，而平面图仅剩Δ(G)=6的开放情形。已知不含4-圈的最大度6平面图是7-全可着色的，本文旨在放宽该结构限制。

Method: 采用图论中的全着色与结构分析技术（如放电法），针对最大度6的平面图，研究其4-圈的结构特征，允许出现某些特殊4-圈而非完全禁止。

Result: 证明了任意最大度为6、不含某些特定4-圈的平面图均存在7-全着色，推广了原有不含4-圈的可着色性结论。

Conclusion: 本文给出了全着色猜想在平面图Δ(G)=6情形下更一般的充分条件，推进了这一开放问题的研究。

Abstract: The Total Coloring Conjecture (TCC) is a challenging unsolved problem posed by Behzad and Vizing independently, which states that every simple graph $G$ admits a ($Δ(G)$ +2)-total-coloring, where $Δ(G)$ denotes the maximum degree of $G$. This conjecture has been confirmed for graphs with $Δ(G)\leq 5$. However, for planar graphs, the only open case is $Δ(G)=6$. It was known that planar graphs with maximum degree 6 and without 4-cycles are 7-totally-colorable. In this paper, we improve this result by showing that any planar graph $G$ of maximum degree 6, which does not contain some special 4-cycles, is 7-totally-colorable.

</details>


### [98] [The smallest square tileable by pairwise incomparable integer rectangles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19536)
*George M. Georgiou*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了最小可用两两不可比的整数矩形铺满的正方形边长为27，即对任意n≤26及k≥2，n×n正方形都无法被k个两两不可比的整数矩形铺砌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Croft、Falconer和Guy在《Unsolved Problems in Geometry》问题C5中给出了27×27正方形由8个两两不可比的整数矩形铺砌的例子，并指出不知道27是否为可被两两不可比整数矩形铺砌的最小正方形边长（铺砌块数量不限）。本文旨在解决这一开放问题，确定该最小边长。

Method: 证明结合了两项结构性归约与对167538个幸存候选铺砌集（candidate tile sets）的穷举搜索，搜索由两个独立编写的程序执行。完整软件、构建说明与输出日志作为附件提供。

Result: 对每个整数n≤26及每个k≥2，n×n正方形都不存在由k个两两不可比的整数矩形构成的铺砌。由此得出27是该边长下界的最小值。

Conclusion: 27是可被两两不可比整数矩形铺砌的最小正方形边长，解决了Problem C5中的开放问题；结果通过结构归约加穷举搜索验证，并经双重独立程序交叉确认。

Abstract: Croft, Falconer and Guy ({Unsolved Problems in Geometry}, Problem~C5) exhibit a tiling of the $27\times27$ square by eight pairwise incomparable integer rectangles and remark that it is not known whether $27$ is the smallest side length of a square that can be tiled by pairwise incomparable integer rectangles, no restriction being placed on the number of tiles. We show that it is: for every integer $n\le26$ and every $k\ge2$, the $n\times n$ square admits no tiling by $k$ pairwise incomparable integer rectangles. The proof combines two structural reductions with an exhaustive search over the $167\,538$ surviving candidate tile sets, carried out by two independently written programs. The complete software, build instructions and output logs are included as ancillary files.

</details>


### [99] [Peeling sequences: a directional method for the three-block construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19540)
*Dániel Gábor Simon*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究有限平面点集的剥离序列计数，证明了一般位置下 n 点集的最小剥离序列数满足 g(n) ≤ g(S_n) = O(6.57^n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剥离序列要求每次移除的点都是剩余点集凸包的顶点。作者希望改进此前对递归三分支构造 S_n 的剥离序列数上界，从而给出 g(n) 更好的上界。

Method: 作者提出一种可用于分析递归三分支构造 S_n 的方法，结合方向限制（directional restrictions）与加权前缀树（weighted prefix-tree）论证来界定 g(S_n)。

Result: 证明了 g(n) ≤ g(S_n) = O(6.57^n)，即对递归三分支构造给出了更优的剥离序列数上界。

Conclusion: 该工作为平面点集剥离序列的最小计数提供了新的上界工具，并改进了已有递归构造的分析结果。

Abstract: A \emph{peeling sequence} of a finite planar point set is an ordering of point removals, in which
  each point is a vertex of the convex hull of the points not yet removed. Write $g(S)$ for the number of such sequences, and $g(n)$ for the minimum of $g(S)$ over $n$-point sets in general position. We present a method which can be used to prove better upper bounds on the previously analysed recursive 3-branch constructions $S_n$. In fact, we prove $g(n)\leq g(S_n)=O(6.57^n)$, using directional restrictions and a weighted prefix-tree argument.

</details>


### [100] [Ordered matchings versus triangles via pseudorandom triangle-free graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19632)
*Wen Chen,Qizhong Lin,Chunlin You*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文证明了随机有序匹配 M 与三角形 K3 的有序 Ramsey 数 r̃(K3, M) 的渐近下界为 Ω(n^{4/3}/(log n)^{1/3})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有序图的 Ramsey 数 r̃(H1,...,Ht)，改进 Balko-Poljak 对随机匹配的下界 Ω((n/log n)^{5/4})，并将 Conlon-Fox-Lee-Sudakov 的一般存在性下界提升 log n 因子。

Method: 结合伪随机无三角形图、保序嵌入的粗略编码，以及由 Brègman 不等式导出的排列回避估计。

Result: 对间隔色数为 2 的 n 顶点均匀随机有序匹配 M，几乎必然有 r̃(K3, M) = Ω(n^{4/3}/(log n)^{1/3})。

Conclusion: 该结果强化了随机匹配情形下的已有下界，并相对于一般存在性下界取得 log n 的改进。

Abstract: For ordered graphs $H_1,\ldots,H_t$, let $\rt(H_1,\ldots,H_t)$ denote the least integer $N$ such that every $t$-coloring of the edges of the naturally ordered complete graph on $[N]$ contains an ordered copy of $H_i$ in color $i$ for some $i\in[t]$. We prove that a uniformly random ordered matching $M$ on $n$ vertices with interval chromatic number two asymptotically almost surely satisfies \[
  \rt(K_3,M)
  =Ω\left(\frac{n^{4/3}}{(\log n)^{1/3}}\right). \] This strengthens the lower bound $Ω((n/\log n)^{5/4})$ of Balko and Poljak for such random matchings and improves the general existential lower bound of Conlon, Fox, Lee and Sudakov by a factor of $\log n$. The proof combines pseudorandom triangle-free graphs, a coarse encoding of order-preserving embeddings, and a permutation avoidance estimate derived from Brègman's inequality.

</details>


### [101] [Unimodality for IDP Lattice Simplices of Prime Normalized Volume](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19637)
*Feihu Liu,Zihao Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明：具有整数分解性质且正规化体积为素数的格单纯形，其 $h^*$-多项式是单峰的，并给出若干单峰的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ferroni 构造了反例，推翻了"具有整数分解性质的格多胞形的 $h^*$-多项式单峰"这一猜想；由此引出问题：具有整数分解性质的格单纯形的 $h^*$-多项式是否仍保持单峰。

Method: 针对具有整数分解性质（IDP）的格单纯形，在正规化体积为素数的条件下研究其 $h^*$-多项式，并进一步推导若干保证单峰性的充分条件。

Result: 证明了任何具有整数分解性质且正规化体积为素数的格单纯形，其 $h^*$-多项式均为单峰。

Conclusion: 在格单纯形这一特殊情形下，即使一般格多胞形的单峰猜想已被 Ferroni 反例否定，素数正规化体积仍可保证 $h^*$-多项式的单峰性，并可由多条充分条件刻画。

Abstract: Recently, Ferroni constructed a family of counterexamples to the well-known conjecture in Ehrhart theory stating that the $h^*$-polynomial of a lattice polytope with the integer decomposition property is unimodal. This raises the question of whether the $h^*$-polynomial of a lattice simplex with the integer decomposition property remains unimodal. In this note, we prove that every lattice simplex with the integer decomposition property and prime normalized volume has a unimodal $h^*$-polynomial. Furthermore, we establish several sufficient conditions for the unimodality of the $h^*$-polynomial of such simplices.

</details>


### [102] [Maximum and Minimum Spectral Radii in an Exceptional Family for Edge-Disjoint Spanning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19655)
*Xuanyu Cao,Chunxiang Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文刻画了图类 $\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}(n,k,\delta)$ 中邻接谱半径的极值图，确定了 $\tau(G)\ge k$ 的尖锐邻接谱阈值，并否定了 Chang–Li–Zhang 猜想2中的匹配型极值构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究边不相交生成树数目 $\tau(G)$ 的谱型充分条件。Chang、Li、Zhang 引入了例外族 $\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}(n,k,\delta)$，并猜想其极大谱半径由匹配型缺失边图给出；本文旨在精确求解该族谱半径极值并验证/修正该猜想，从而给出尖锐阈值。

Method: 采用精确的 core–placement（核–放置）参数化，并配合一致的 Schur 补预解式展开。分析中第一个依赖候选的系数是关于 $\sum_{x\in V(M)}d_M(x)^2$ 的仿射函数，第一个对放置敏感的系数是平方负载泛函；利用等配商矩阵（equitable quotient matrices）求出两个极值谱半径。

Result: 对固定可行 $(k,\delta)$ 及充分大 $n$，确定了族内邻接谱半径的最大值与最小值：当 $h=\delta-k\ge2$ 时，唯一极大图（同构意义下）满足 $M\cong K_{1,h}\cup(h+1)K_1$，例外边嵌套于大团一侧；对任意 $h\ge1$，唯一极小图满足 $M\cong hK_2\cup2K_1$，$2h$ 条例外边在大团一侧端点互异。还解决了 $h=0,1$ 情形及全部等号情形。

Conclusion: Chang–Li–Zhang 猜想2提出的匹配型配置并非极值；极大图给出了 $\tau(G)\ge k$ 的全局尖锐邻接谱阈值，修正了原有猜想并完备了该例外族的谱极值刻画。

Abstract: For a connected graph $G$, let $τ(G)$ denote the maximum number of pairwise edge-disjoint spanning trees, and let $ρ(G)$ be its adjacency spectral radius. For integers $n\ge1$, $k\ge2$, and $k\leδ\le2k-1$, let $\mathcal{G}_{n,δ}$ be the class of connected $n$-vertex graphs with minimum degree $δ$, and let $\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}(n,k,δ)\subseteq\mathcal{G}_{n,δ}$ be the exceptional family introduced by Chang, Li, and Zhang. For sufficiently large $n$, the sharp adjacency-spectral threshold for $τ(G)\ge k$ is determined by maximizing $ρ(G)$ over this family.
  Set $h=δ-k$. For each fixed admissible pair $(k,δ)$ and all sufficiently large $n$, we determine the maximum and minimum adjacency spectral radii in $\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}(n,k,δ)$. In the core--placement representation, let $M$ be the missing-edge graph of the bounded core. For $h\ge2$, the unique maximizer, up to isomorphism, satisfies $M\cong K_{1,h}\cup(h+1)K_1$, with the exceptional edges nested on the large-clique side. Hence the matching configuration proposed in Conjecture~2 of Chang--Li--Zhang is not extremal. For every $h\ge1$, the unique minimizer satisfies $M\cong hK_2\cup2K_1$, with the $2h$ exceptional edges having distinct endpoints on the large-clique side. We also settle the cases $h=0,1$ and all equality cases.
  The proof combines an exact core--placement parametrization with a uniform Schur-complement resolvent expansion. The first candidate-dependent coefficient is affine in $\sum_{x\in V(M)}d_M(x)^2$, while the first placement-sensitive coefficient is a squared-load functional. Equitable quotient matrices yield the two extremal radii, and the maximizing graph gives the sharp global adjacency-spectral threshold.

</details>


### [103] [On integral polytopes related to Edmonds' problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19703)
*Hiroshi Hirai*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文从多面体视角研究交换与非交换Edmonds问题，将线性符号矩阵的秩计算转化为整多面体P(A)与Q(A)上的线性优化，建立了整多面体层级并给出整性间隙下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交换与非交换Edmonds问题是计算线性符号矩阵秩的经典问题，此前关于nc-rank已有若干结果，但从多面体与线性优化角度统一刻画二者关系、刻画交换松弛与非交换多面体之间的差距仍不充分，作者希望借此建立层级结构并量化整性间隙。

Method: 将交换与非交换Edmonds问题分别视为整多面体P(A)与Q(A)上的线性优化；P(A)与Q(A)分别由A的子行列式及其blow-up A^{d}的子行列式的指数向量凸包得到；通过推广已有nc-rank结果构造多面体层级P(A)⊆P^{≤2}(A)⊆P^{≤3}(A)⊆…=Q(A)，并分析整性间隙。

Result: 建立了整多面体层级P(A)⊆P^{≤2}(A)⊆P^{≤3}(A)⊆…=Q(A)；证明Q(A)相对于P(A)的整性间隙至少为1/2；进一步证明当每个A_k为秩2反对称矩阵时，该层级在第二层终止且整性间隙提升至2/3。

Conclusion: 交换与非交换Edmonds问题可通过整多面体层级统一刻画，非交换多面体Q(A)相对交换松弛P(A)存在至少1/2的整性间隙，且在秩2反对称特例下层级有限终止、间隙改进为2/3。

Abstract: In this paper, we study polyhedral aspects on commutative and noncommutative Edmonds' problems for computing the rank of linear symbolic matrix $A = \sum_{k=1}^m A_k x_k$. We regard them as linear optimization over integral polytopes ${\cal P}(A)$ and ${\cal Q}(A)$, respectively, which are obtained by the convex hulls of exponent vectors of subdeterminants of~$A$ and its blow-ups $A^{\{d\}} = \sum_{k=1}^m A_k \otimes X_k$ $(d=1,2,\ldots)$. By extending previously known results on nc-rank, we establish a hierarchy of integral polytopes ${\cal P}(A) \subseteq {\cal P}^{\leq 2}(A) \subseteq {\cal P}^{\leq 3}(A) \subseteq \cdots = {\cal Q}(A)$ and show that the integrality gap of ${\cal Q}(A)$ relative to ${\cal P}(A)$ is at least $1/2$. Further, we show that if each $A_k$ is rank-2 skew-symmetric, then the above hierarchy terminates at the second level and the integrality gap is improved to $2/3$.

</details>


### [104] [Paths maximize the expected range of graph-indexed random walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19728)
*Yinfeng Zhu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文证明了在给定阶数下，路径最大化向整数作图的均匀图同态的期望range，从而建立BHM猜想的期望形式；并证明BHM不等式蕴含LNR不等式，得到LNR猜想的一推论；结果经Lean 4形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定理核心动机是建立Benjamini–Häggström–Mossel（BHM）猜想的期望形式，并进一步导出Loebl–Nešetřil–Reed（LNR）猜想。

Method: 方法：对每个二分部类上的同态进行限制与重标定（restrict and rescale），再收缩高度函数取常数的边；对零边（zero edges）的秩作定量估计，以补偿简单随机游走期望range中的奇偶性项，从而实现对顶点数的归纳。

Result: 结果：证明路径在所有同阶连通二分图中最大化均匀选取的整数值图同态的期望range（一端固定为零），确立了BHM猜想的期望形式；并证明BHM不等式蕴含任意连通图上均匀整数1-Lipschitz函数的LNR不等式，故LNR猜想是BHM的推论。

Conclusion: 结论：BHM期望形式成立并蕴含LNR猜想；证明通过作者与AI交互获得并由作者验证，主要结果已在Lean 4中形式化并检查通过。

Abstract: We prove that a path maximizes the expected range of a uniformly chosen graph homomorphism into the integers, with one vertex pinned at zero, among all connected bipartite graphs of the same order. This establishes the expectation form of the Benjamini--Häggström--Mossel conjecture.
